		cost = 0
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SYR_citizenship_entry_pending
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SYR_citizenship_entry_implemented }
		}

		available = {
			hidden_trigger = { original_tag = SYR IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes }
		}
		complete_effect = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = COLDWAR_SYR_citizenship_programme }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_SYR_citizenship_programme_stability_factor = 0.005 tooltip = stability_factor_tt }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_SYR_citizenship_programme_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier = 0.01 tooltip = bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt }
				unlock_decision_tooltip = COLDWAR_SYR_decision_arab_belt
			}
			hidden_effect = { COLDWAR_SYR_citizenship_entry_implement = yes }
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 1 }
	}
